5a1(ka): @#//nyon mongs pa med pa/dbang dang ldan par gyur pa/sems shin du rnam par grol ba 
5a2(ka): shes rab shin du rnam par grol ba/cang shes pa/glang po chen po bya ba byas pa/byed pa byas pa/
5a3(ka): khur bor ba/bdag gi don rab tu thob pa/srid par kun du sbyor ba/yongs su zad pa/yang dag pa'i 
5a4(ka): shes pas/sems shin du rnam par 'grol ba/sems kyi dbang thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol du 
5b1(ka): son pa sha stag dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' khri'i byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chen po la 'di lta ste//lhun po'i
5b2(ka): rtse 'dzin gzhon nur gyur pa dang /chu lha'i blo gros gzhon nur gyur pa dang/bzang skyong blo gros gzhon nur gyur ba dang /rgyal ba'i blo gros
5b3(ka): gzhon nur gyur pa dang 'od kyi blo gros gzhon nur gyur dang /nam mkha'i blo gros gzhon nur gyu ba dang /glog gi blo gros gzhon nur gyur
5b4(ka): pa dang /'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang thub dka' gzhon nur gyur pa dang/chu lha gzhon nur gyur pa dang dri ma med pa gzhon nur gyur
5b5(ka): pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /de dag lasogs pa/byang chub sems dpa' khri thams cad kyang /bzod
6a1(ka): @#//dang gzungs dang /ting nge 'dzin thob pa/chos thams cad la sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes can/sems can thams cad la
6a2(ka): sems mnyam pa/bdud kyi yul thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul la 'jug pa/
6a3(ka): shes pa la mkhas pa/byams pa chen po dang /snying rje chen po dang ldan pa thabs shes pa la mkhas pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/de'i
6a4(ka): tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po na/kun du rgyu mkhas pa gsal ba yid gzhungs pa/rigs pa'i gnas bcwo brgyad kyi pha rol du phyin 
6a5(ka): pa g.yog lnga brgya pa gcig gi ming ni nye rgyal/gcig gi ming ni pang nas skyes shes bya ba gnyis shig 'khod te/tshogs kyi gtso bo/g.yog
6b1(ka): gi tshogs can de gnyis phan tshun 'di ltar bdag cag las gang gis thog mar bdud rtsi thob pa des/gcig la gcig sbran to/zhes dam bcas so//de nas tshe dang ldan pa
6b2(ka): rta thul snga dro'i dus kyi tshe sham thabs dang chos gos bgos/lhung bzed thogs nas/rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen por bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /de
6b3(ka): nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal gyis/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul de lta bu'i spyod yul du zhugs nas/rgyal po'i khab gyi grong khyer chen por bsod snyams kyi phyir song ba mthong ngo //mthong
6b4(ka): nas de 'di snyam du sems te/ji ltar dge sbyong 'di'i spyod lam mdzes pa/de lta bu ni bdag gis sngon chad dge sbyong ngam/bram ze 'am/mir gyur pa gzhan su gang la
6b5(ka): yang sngon ma mthong na/bdag de'i gnas su song ste/tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyi ston pa gang yin/gang las rab tu byung /gang gi chos la dga' zhes dri'i snyam nas/de nas kun
6b6(ka): du rgyu nye rgyal tshe dang ldan pa rta thul ga la bder song ste phyin nas/tshe dang ldan pa rta thuladang lhan cig mngon du yang dag par dga' bar bya ba dang /mgu bar bya
7a1(ka): @#//ba'i gtam sna tshogs byas te/phyogs gcig tu 'dug go//phyogs gcig tu 'duganas kyang /kun du rgyu nye rgyal gyis tshe dang ldan pa rta thul la 'di 
7a2(ka): skad ces smras so//tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyi ston pa gang /gang las rab tu byung /gang gi chos la dga'/de nas tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyis/kun du rgyu nye rgyal la 'di 
7a3(ka): skad ces smras sa//shAkya'i sras po brtul zhugs bka' thub chen po kun gyi dmang dbang ldan pa//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho pha rol phyin cing grol la de bzhin 'gro ba grol 
7a4(ka): mdzad pa'i//sangs rgyas shes bya sad mdzad ma mnyam sdug bsngal mtsha skyems de ltar 'di na yod//dri ma med pa de yi skyabs su nga song de yi chos la mngon par dgA 
7a5(ka): nye rgyal gyis smras pa/khyod kyi ston pa ji skad smra/ji skad ston/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyi sa smras pa/de'i phyir tshe dang ldan pa legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig 
7a6(ka): dngangs bshad do//ji ltar 'jig rten las dang nyon mongs rgyu ba bcas byed rgyur ldan 'byung dang //las dang nyon mongs pa dag ldog rgyu de yang 'dran pas rab 
7b1(ka): du gsungs//gang na skye dang rga dang rgud pa'i sdug bsngal nges par mi gnas pa//thar pa'i mchog de smra ba'i khyu mchog de yis rang gis mkhyen te gsungs//de nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal gyis
7b2(ka): chos kyi rnam grangs 'di thos nas/chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul med cing dri ma dang bral la/nyon mongs pa med bskyes te/des rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu thob nas/de'i
7b3(ka): tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di skad ces smras so//sangs rgyas kyis ni shin du rnyed dka' chos gter bdud rtsi'i chos bshad pa//'di ni rtag tu skye ba'i 'bab chu skyem par bden par khong
7b4(ka): du chud||de ni 'gro ba rnams kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar 'gyur ba ste//lam 'di dam pa shes rab chos rnams sgom pa mi mnyam byed pa yin//de nas kun du rgyu nyer rgyal gyis/tshe
7b5(ka): dang ldan pa rta thul la 'di skad ces smras so/tshe dang ldan pa de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de da ltar ga la bzhugs/tshe dang ldan pa rta
7b6(ka): thul gyis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa bcom ldan 'das ni rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal bye ka lan d+ha ka gnas pa na/'di lta ste|ral pa can stong rab tu byung ba'i dge slong gi dge
7b7(ka): 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//nye rgyal gyis smras pa/bdag gis grogs po'i mchog dang /slob ma'i 'khor la bltas te/bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du rab
8a1(ka): @#//du byung ngo snyam nas/de nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal gyis tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyi rkang pa la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas/lan gsum bskor ba byas te/slar log nas mang nas 
8a2(ka): skyes ga la bder song ba dang /kun du rgyu bar nas skyes kyis/kun du rgyu nye rgyal brgyang ring po nas long ba mthong ste/mthong nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal la 'di skad ces smras so//tshe dang 
8a3(ka): ldan pa khyod kyi dbang po rnams ni dangs/gzhin gyi mdangs ni yongs su dag/lpags pa'i mdog ni kun du dkar ba lags na/tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyis bdud rtsi rnyed do//nye rgyal gyis 
8a4(ka): smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa de de bzhin te/kho bos bdud rtsi rnyed kyis/tshe dang ldan pa de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang kho bos ci rnyed pa khyod la bshad do//de nas 
8a5(ka): kun du rgyu bang nas skyes stan las langs nas/blo gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa btsugs nas/kun du rgyu nya rgyal ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar 
8a6(ka): ba btud de/'di skad ces smras so//gang gis srid gsum rgya mtsho myur rgal zhing //phung po dgra chen rnam par gzhig bya ba//mya ngan med zhi lam de shad cig dang //yang byung med 
8a7(ka): pa'i lam du 'jug par bya//de nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal gyis 'di skad ces smras so//ji ltar 'jig rten las dang nyon mongs rgyur bcas byed rgyur ldan 'byung dang //las dang nyon 
8b1(ka): mongs pa dag ldog rgyud yang 'dren pas rab tu gsungs//gang na skye dang rga dang rgud pa'i sdug bsngal nges par mi gnas pa//thar pa'i mchog te smra ba'i khyu mchog de yis rang gis mkhyen te 
8b2(ka): gsungs//pang nas skyes kyis smras pa/sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar bya phyir dri ma med pa zhi ba'i chos//nyon mongs lta ngan nyes pa thams cad zhi byed mi shes gcod pa dang //'di ni 'dus 
8b3(ka): byas gsog gsob de phyir stong par slu bar thub pas gsungs//de thos zhi ba rig 'gyur dri ma med pa'i tshig de zlos te gsungs//nye rgyal gyis smras pa/ji ltar 'jig rten las dang nyon mongs 
8b4(ka): rgyur bcas byed rgyur ldan 'byung dge las dang nyon mongs pa dag ldog rgyu de yang 'dren pas rab tu gsangs//gang na skye dang rga ba dang rgud pa'i sdug bsngags pas par mi gnas pa//thal ba'i mchog 
8b5(ka): de smra ba'i khyu mchog de yi rang gis mkhyen te gsungs//de nas kun du rgyu pang nas skyes kyis de nyid du chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul med cing dri ma dang bral la nyon mongs pa med pa 
8b6(ka): rnam par sbyangs te/des rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu thob nas 'di skad ces smras so//yang dag spyod pa 'di ni chu bo sgrol ba zhi ba mgyogs pa'i gzings//ye shes mchog 'di sdug bsngal 
8b7(ka): gsum po zhi byed 'khor ba'i pha rol 'gro//'di ni yongs su shes na nyon mongs phung po 'joms zhing bdud 'dul 'gyur//thar pa 'di ni dgra dang thabs bsal cing sdug bsngal rgya 
9a1(ka): @#//mtsho bskyems//pang na skyes kyis smras pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de de ltar ga la bzhug+sa
9a2(ka): nye rgyal gyis sras pa/tshe dang ldan pa bdag gis bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan d+ha ka gnas phan 'di lta ste ral pa can stong
9a3(ka): rab tu byung ba'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun chen po dang /thabs gcig tu bzhugs zhes thos nas/bdag cag gnyis bcom ldan
9a4(ka): 'das de'i thad du rab tu byung ngam/pang nas skyes kyis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa de de bzhin te/bdag cag gi 'khor la bltas la rab tu byung ngo //de nas kun du nye rgyal dang /
9a5(ka): pang nas skyes kyis rang gi 'khor ga la ba der dong ngo //de nas skad cig thang cig yud tsam de la bdud sdig can gyis yul ang ga mang ga d+ha na mkhas shing snyan pa dang /brjod pa dang grag+sa
9a6(ka): par ldan pa/skyes bu dam pa nye rgyal dang /pang nas skyes g.yog dang bcas te/dge sbyong ko'u ta ma'i bstan pa la rab tu byung bar 'dod do zhes thos nas/de 'di snyam du sems so//
9a7(ka): kyi hud de gnyis gal te dge sbyong go'u ta ma'i slob mar gyur na/bdag gi bdud kyi yul stongs par byed kyis bdag der song ste/skyes bu dam pa de gnyis rab tu 'byung ba las zlog la/
9b1(ka): sdig pa can gyi lta ba 'dzin du gzhug go snyam ste/de nas skad cig thang cig yud tsam de la bdud sdig can/myur du bdag gi khang pa nas mi snang bar byas te/che dang ldan pa rta thul gyi cha byad
9b2(ka): dang rtags dang /spyod lam du bsgyur nas/skyes bu dam pa de gnyis kyi mdun na/lam khar 'dug ste 'di skad ces smras so//kho bos sngar smras rgyu dang byed rgyu dang lta bu de ni log pa ste/khyed
9b3(ka): gnyis yid kyi spyod pa ci la nges pa bgam khar bya ba'i phyir/smras pa de dag thams cad don yod ma yin 'di la rgyu yang med//dge dang mi dge las de'i 'bras bu thob pa 'di na ga la yod
9b4(ka): myur du khyed gnyis 'dod pa'i yon tan rnams la rab spyod rtsed mo byos//skye rga na 'chi med cing sdug bsngal med de 'jig rten pha rol me//bsod nams ma yin las skyes 'bras bu
9b5(ka): rgyu dang byed pa med//rnyed phyir 'dir ni shAkya'i bu yis smras kyis dad pas ma 'gro shig/de nas nye rgyal dang pang nas skyes gnyis 'di snyam du sems te/kye ma bdud sdig can bdag
9b6(ka): cag rab tu byung ba las bzlog pa'i phyir 'ongs so snyam nas/de nas nye rgyal rgyab kyis phyogs te 'dug nas/rang gi 'khor la bos te 'di skad ces smras so//bram ze'i khye'u nyon cig
9b7(ka): 'khor ba'i skyon//dran par gyis la 'jig rten rga ba yis//gzir cing 'chi bdag gis ni rab bskor ba//de gnyis spang phyir rab byung legs par zung //de nas phang nas skyes gyis
10a1(ka): @#//bdud la slar smras pa//mkhas pa'i blo 'chang sdug bsngal gsum mthar phyin mchog chos rnams kun shes kyis//rnam pa kun du bdag cag blo gros 'di las bskyod nus su yang 
10a2(ka): med/sred pa rab zhi bya phyir bdag cag brtan pa'i yid kyis rtag tu 'bad//seng ge'i gzugs can wa yi tshig gis nged kyi blo gros ma bsgyur cig/bden pa mthong ba'i lha gang dag nam/
10a3(ka): mkha' la 'dug pa de dag gis//skyes bu dam pa de gnyis la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/skyes bu dam pa khyed legs so legs so//'di lta ste/rab tu byung ba dang /mngon par byung ba 'di ni 
10a4(ka): 'jig rten thams cad las khyad par du 'phags pa'i lam yin te/lam 'di ni sdug bsngal thams cad zhi ba'o//lam 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul la 'jug 
10a5(ka): pa'o/'di lta ste/rab tu byung bya dang /mngon par 'byung ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyis rab tu sngags shing bstod pa'i lam mo//de nas bdud sdig can sdug 
10a6(ka): bsngal zhing yid mi bde ste/yid la gcags te de nas de nyid du mi snang bar song ngo //de nas kun du rgyu nye rgyal dang pang nas skyes kyis rang gi 'khor la bltas te 'di skad ces smras so/
10a7(ka): bram ze'i khye'u dag nged ni rga dang na ba dang 'chi ba'i rgya mtsho'i pha rol tu 'gro ba'i phyir//de bzhin gshegs pa las zab tu byung bar yang dag par chas kyis khyed kyis shes par gyis 
10b1(ka): shig khyed rnams las gang bcom ldan 'das kyi bstan pa la rab tu 'byung mi 'dod pa de ni 'di nas phyir lo gshig/de nas bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya po de dag thams cad kyis 'di skad ces smros
10b2(ka): bdag cag cung zad shes pa de kun kyang khyed kyi mthun/khyed rgya chen po'i gnas su rab tu byung na/khyed gang las rab tu 'byung ba de las bdag kyang rab tu 'byung ngo//de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal dang
10b3(ka): pang nas skyes g.yog lnga brgya dang bcas pa bcom ldan 'das las rab tu byung bar chas par bdud sdig can gyis rig nas/ci nas de gnyis dge sbyong ke'u ta ma'i gam du 'gro mi nus par
10b4(ka): bya ba'i phyir/rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po'i phyi logs su g.yangs chen po zabasu dpag tshad brgya pa zhig sprul to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang 'di 'dra ba'i rdzu 'phrul mngon
10b5(ka): par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad de/ci nas kun tu nye rgyal dang pang nas skyes gnyis kyis g.yangs chen po de mi mthong zhing /lam drang por 'gro bar mdzad do//yang bdud sdig can
10b6(ka): gyis de gnyis kyi mdun du/ri chen po brtan pa sra ba ma rnyil ba/su la med pa gcig tu stug por gyur pa/mkhregs pa 'phang du dpag tshad stong yod pa mngon par sprul te/seng ge khro zhing
10b7(ka): gdug pa gtum pa/sgra chen po 'byin pa stong yang mngon par sprul na/bcom ldan 'das kyi gzi brjid dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus/skyes bu dam pa de gnyis kyis ri de yang ma mthong /
11a1(ka): @#//gsang ge yang ma mthong /seng ge'i skad kyang ma thos te/lam drang por bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su song ngo //bcom ldan 'das ni 'khor mang po brgya stong du mas yong 
11a2(ka): su bskor cing /mdun gyis bltas nas chos ston to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' bstsal pa/dge slong dag khyed skyes ba dam pa/tshogs kyi gtso bo g.yog gi tshogs 
11a3(ka): can 'di gnyis la bltos/de dag gis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das mthong lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/dge slongs dag skyes bu dam pa 'di gnyis g.yog dang bcas 
11a4(ka): te|nga'i thad du rab tu byung nas/gcig ni nga'i nyan thos thams cad kyi nang na/shes rab can gyi mchog tu 'gyur ro//gcig ni rdzu 'phrul can gyi mchog tu 'gyur ro //de nas dge slong zhig gis 
11a5(ka): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di smras so//skyes bu mkhas pa g.yog ldan 'di gnyis ni//rdzu 'phrul ldan dang shes rab 'jigs med par//mi mchog phan pa mdzad pas lung bstan te//
11a6(ka): bdag gis de gnyis bsti bstong bskyed nas gsu/de nas dge slong de stan las langs te/dge slong mang po dang /byim na gnas pa dang /rab tu byung ba mang po dang lhan cig tu skyes bu dam 
11a7(ka): pa de gnyis bsu nas bsnyen bkur byas so//de nas skyes bu dam pa de gnyis/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/
11b1(ka): ldan gsum bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug ste/bcom ldan 'das li 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rab tu byung ba dang /bsnyen pa rdzogs pa dge slong gi
11b2(ka): dngos po bdag cag gnyis la bstsal du gsol/bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyi thad chungs par spyad pa spyod do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/rigs kyi bu khyed gnyis kyi ming ci nye rgyal
11b3(ka): gis gsol pa/bdag ni bram ze nye rgyal gyi bu lags te/nye rgyal zhes bgyi/bdag gi ma'i ming ni sha ri ka ste/bdag de las skyes pas na/de'i slad du bdag gi ming ni sha ri'i bu zhes btags so//
11b4(ka): bdag rab tu byung bar yang pha mas gnang lags so//pang nas skyes kyis gsol pa/bdag gi pha'i ming ni ke'u ti n+ya zhes bgyi/bdag gi ma'i mngi ni mo'u 'gal zhes bgyi ste/bdag de las skyes
11b5(ka): pas na/de'i slad du bdagagi ming ni mo'u 'gal gyi bu zhes btags so//bdag rab tu byung bar yangapha mas gnang lags so/bcom ldan 'das kyis shA ri'i bu dang /mo'u 'gal gyi bu gnyis
11b6(ka): nga'i thad du g.yog dang bcas te/tshangs par spyad pa spyod cig ces bka' bstsal pa dang /de nyid kyis de gnyis rab tu byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs shing dge slong gi dngos por gyur to//shA ri'i bu dang
11b7(ka): mo'u 'gal gyi bu g.yog dang bcas pa rab tu byung nas/ring po ma lon par bdud sdig can dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs su/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug nas 'di skad ces smras so//gang dag bstan
12a1(ka): @#//bcos don spyod mkhas pa rig pa'i pha rol song //de kun nga yi rkang la phyag 'tshal de dag 'dren pa pa//ko'u ta ma slob ma 'khor bcas ngal deng myur skyabs tshol cig 
12a2(ka): mya ngan 'das 'gro rgyas pa bsal ba'i lam ni khyod la bshad//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa//khyod lam 'gro ba 'di dag ngan 'gro 'dren cing sdug bsngal rgya mtsho rnyed par 
12a3(ka): byed|nga yi lam na rgyu dang mi rgyu 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal rgya mtsho skyems byed pa//'tshal pa 'dzem bag med cing mu co smra ba wa skad da yang ci zhig zer/khyod ni bcom zin bdud kyi las kyis ngal nga yang 
12a4(ka): 'dir ni byed mi nus//de nas bdud sdig can gyis dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs mi snang bar byas nas/tshangs pa'i cha byad du bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug ste/'di skad ces smras so//khyod 
12a5(ka): kyi shes rab kyis ni las dang nyon mongs srid pa'i myu gu rab tu bsal//de yi slan chad thub pa ci 'dra sems can don 'dis 'di ltar sdug bsngal spro//gtso bo 'gro ba 'di na khyod kyi 
12a6(ka): snod 'gyur skye bo gang yang ma mchis te//dus 'di lags na skyon dang bral khyod ci yi slad du mya ngan myur mi 'da'//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsal pa/ngas 'dul gang 'khod sems 
12a7(ka): can mtshungs med gang ga'i bye ma snyed//ngas mthong de dag nga yi snying rjes thar bar bya yin//'gro ba rab 'bring ngan par gyur pa thar te mthar thug na//de nas mya ngan 'das yis blo gros ngan pa 
12b1(ka): go yos tsi bskul//de nas yang bdud sdig can sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde nas yid la bcags te/de nyid du mi snang bar gyur nas rang gi gnas su song ste/mya ngan gyi khang par zhugs te 
12b2(ka): 'dug pa dang de'i mod la bdud kyi gnas na 'khod pa'i sems can kun phan tshun 'di skad du ci'i rgyus bdag gi rgyal po chen po 'di mya ngan gyi khang bar zhugs te 'dug pa sus kyang mi shes so

53a3(ka): po de dag de nyid du 'dus par gyur te/de dag la 'di skad ces smras so//sa 'dir bye ba'i bdud rnams nyer lhags kyang //thub ba rlabs chen thugs mnga' nam yang ni//ma rung mi 'gyur 
53a4(ka): khyed cag ma 'jigs shig//dge ba 'di la bdag cag mngon sum gyur//dpag tshad sum bcu drug du mgyogs shing drag shul ldan pas kun tu bskor//rna bra ral gri mtshon don rtsub pa'i

53a6(ka): nas lha mo gzhan zhig du zhing 'di skad ces smras so//sngon byung ba ni bdud kyi sde cig yin te de dag dpung mi che//'di ni bye ba khrag khrig bdud kyi sde ste shin tu dpung che bas//'jig rten rnam 
53a7(ka): par 'dren pa 'dir ni brlag par gyur du rab tu dogs//de brlag na ni 'jig rten 'di dag shin tu snang med 'khyams par 'ong //de nas brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba 
53b1(ka): de dag gis/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la 'jus nas/'di skad ces gsol te/blo zhan bdag cag gi ni tshig gson te/dge ba bzhugs shig gtso bo thugs rje can//lha mang bye ba
53b2(ka): mya ngan chen pos gdungs//de la chos kyi bcud kyis dag tor cig//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad dang ldan ba'i 'khor de dag la/byams pa yangs pa'i spyan gyis gzigs nas
53b3(ka): de dag shin tu dbugs 'byung ba'i phyir/tshangs pa'i dbyangs kyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//deng khyed ma 'jigs khyed ni ma skrag cig//bdud rnams thams cad bzhon cas cig car du//
53b4(ka): tshogs kyang spu gcig skrag par mi nus na//nga yi lus kun la ni smos ci dgos//deng ni 'jig rten thams cad dbugs dbyung ste//nga yis sa 'dir rtag tu chos 'chad do//lam rnams ston pa
53b5(ka): la ni nga nyid kyis//dri ma med pa yang dag la ma bstan bya//ngas sngon bya ba dka' ba mang po byas//zas dang skom yang shin tu rgya char byin//gnas dang sman yang mi nyung sbyin byin bas//nga la deng
53b6(ka): su gnod par byed par nus//ngas ni rta dang glang chen shing rta dang//lha ba lhub phyang 'phrul dag gis brgyan rnams dang//bran pho bran mo grong brdal yul 'khor btang//nga lo su zhig gnod par
53b7(ka): byed par nus//chung ma bu pho bu mo ban mang dang//yid 'ong dbang phyug sa steng rgyal po'i rigs//sems can rnams la phan phyir ngas byin gyis//nga lus di ring ci phyir 'jigs par 'gyur//
54a1(ka): @#//mgo dang sna dang rna ba mig gnyis dang /lus dang lpags pa khrag dang rkang lag bcas//nga yi srog kyang lus can rnams la btang //su zhig nga la che bar byed par nus//
54a2(ka): nga ni sangs rgyas bye ba rab mang la//gus ldan bdag gis lag nas shin du mchod//rtag tu tshul khrims thos dang bzod la dga'//nga la su zhig brlag par byed par nus//ngas sngon 
54a3(ka): shin du byad ka' mang po rnams//mnyam par bzhag pas rab tu nga yis byas//lus kun bcad kyang nga la 'khrug med na//nga la rnam par gtsa bar su zhig nus//nyon mongs ngas brtul

55a1(ka): @#//dang mi dag gis btub pa dang /mi tog dang /spos dang /phreng ba dang /byug pa dang /lha ba lhu ba dang /rgyan sna tshogs kyis bcom ldan 'das la mngon par mchod nas /bcom ldan

60a3(ka): bzhin du 'dug nas/ting nge 'dzin thams cad la blta zhing /gzhan la rag las pa med de/ting nge 'dzin thams can kyis mi 'phrogs par 'gyur ba/byang chub sems dpa' ting nge

60a5(ka): 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do//yon tan mtha' yas 'jig rten dgon pa bden pa gsung ba legs bzhugs pa/khyod kyi ye shes spyan kyi 'od zer 
60a6(ka): gyis ni 'jig rten kun snang mdzad//rtag tu sems can kun gyi slad du dpa' bo byams pas mngon 'phags pa/sems can snying po chags pa zad pa 'dren pa khyod la deng phyag

60b2(ka): rab brgyan pa//sems can snying po sangs rgyas 'gyur ba 'dren pa de ring bshad du gsol//bdag gis lta ba'i rgya mtsho bcad cing grol nas 'gro ba bsgral bar bgyi//ji srid kyis ni sangs 
60b3(ka): rgyas 'gyur ba mi yi dam pa lung bstan gsol//sdug bsngal chu bos bdas pa sems can srid pa'i mtsho las bsgral bar bgyi//mya ngan 'das pa'i bde ba zag pa med pa'i lam la 'god 
60b4(ka): par bgyi//phyogs bcu dag na sangs rgyas gang rnams yon tan rgya mtsho lta bu dag//de dag bdag gis dpang du gsol te byang chub tu ni sems gzhol gyis//drag cing mi bzad chu'i 
60b5(ka): pa'i sdug bsngal dag gis reg pa'i sems can kun//dus gsum du ni bdag gis lus dang ngag dang yid kyis bsod nams des//sems can thams cad sdug bsngal de ring zhi bar 
60b6(ka): gyur cig sems can kun//sangs rgyas kyi ni bde dang ldan zhing rtag tu mya ngan 'das par shog//sems can thams can de'i gyur cig nyon mongs chu yang skams par shog//ngo bo nyid 
60b7(ka): kyis ye shes dbang po sangs rgyas po len pa thob gyur cig//sems can gang dag bsad par chad cing sag bsngal dag gis gtses pa rnams//de dag bdag gis bsod nams gzi brjid 
61a1(ka): @#//ngag gis myur du nya bar shog//sems can re res kyang ni yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad thob gyur cig//shes rab bsod nams tshad med pa dang bde ba kun gyis tshim par

61a3(ka): pa'i rgya mtsho pha rol rgal gyur cig//thams cad chos kyi pha rol phyin pa sangs rgyas su yang grub par shog//bskal pa dpag tu med par chos kyi cha rab 'bebs shing 'dug gyur cig/

7a3(kha): rgyal po chen po yul 'khor bsrung stan las langs te/bla g+sa phrag pa gcig la gzar na/pus mo g.yas pI lha nga sa la btsugs te/bac+dos ga la ba de logs thal maM sbyar na/bac+dos la 'di ka
7a4(kha): skad ces gsol to//bac+dos bdag gi 'khor de zi' gdon gyis btab pa'i tshul gyi mtshan ma ni 'di lta bu lag+sa te/glu dag len cing bro rdungs/rgyan rnaMs la ni dgA ba bgyid//pharki pa ma mchis rnad
7a5(kha): par smra//dgod par bgyid cing khro ba 'gyur//skoM par gyur la mig dam ste//rtag tu rims kyis 'debs par bgyid//mig kyang 'byed par mi bgyid de//rgyab kyis phyog shing nyal bar 'gyed//de la srang
7a6(kha): kyi tshig mchis kyis 'jig rten mgon po bdag la gson//s+yad yA tha dan/a kha ma kha dbyin khe/ben d+h+ye/bar te/tsa pa la/ba khe ba kha ne/a khe li/du ba hU la/ba hu li/ba gan da li/ba sha ba
7a7(kha): de swAhA/rgyal po chen po yul 'khor bsrung gi ming dang stobs dang/dbang phyug dang/byin gyis bdag gdon thaMd dang/gnod pa thaMd las thar bar gyur cig/swA hA/de nas rgyal po chen po 'phag+sa skyes
7b1(kha): po stan las langs te/bla gas phrag pa gcig tu gzer nas/pus mo g.yas pa la ngas la btsugs te/bac+dos ga la ba der logs su thal mo sbyar na/bac+dos nyid la phyag 'tshal zhing 'di skad ces 
7b2(kha): bac+dos bdag gi 'khor yi dgas grul bu gyi gdon gyis zin pa'i tshul gyi mtshan ma ni 'di lta bu lags te/shin tu skom pas 'debs par 'gyur//g.yeng bas bteb cing lta bar 'gyur//gzhin yang dam po gda'
7b3(kha): bar 'gyur//bskums nas la nyal bar 'gyur//kha dog ngan cing lus kyang skye ma//skra dang sen mo ring ba ste//dri ma can la dri mi gda'//rdzun dang khyal pa smra bar bgyid//de la sngags kyi tshig mchis kyis
7b4(kha): 'jig rten mgon po bdag la gson//sad ya tha dan/kha kha kha me/kha la ni/kha lo me/kha ra li/kha ra le/kar te/ka ra khe/ka shi ni/ka ra te/ka mi ni/bi ma le/byi shi yi sha ba ti/sha mi sha mi
7b5(kha): na swAhA/rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po'i ming der/stobs dang dbang phyug dang byin gyis bdag gi gdon dang /gnod pa thams cad zhi bar gyur cig/swAhA/de na rgyal po chen po mig mi bzang stan las
7b6(kha): langs te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bac+dos ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas/bac+dos nyid la phyag 'tshal te/'di skad ces gsol te//bac+dos bdag
7b7(kha): la sngogs 'dab bzangs kyi gdon gyis btab pa'i tshal gyi mtshan ma ni 'di lta ba lags te/skyigs bus 'debs shing dbugs kyang 'byin/dpe zhi dbugs kyang grang bar 'gyur//rdul zhi lbu ba kha chu 'byang phyi phyir gnyis kyang log par 'gyur te/mdog la
8a1(kha): @#//de bzhin 'dar zhing rgyug par 'gyur//sen mo dag kyang ston par bgyid//dog sar mchis shing du bar bgyid//de la sngags kyi tshig mchis kyis//
8a2(kha): 'jig rten mgon po bdag la gson//sang yA the dan/kra ka ya/kra ka she/kra ga se/kru sha me/kru ge/krug ka me/kru ge/a ga le/na ga le/sa
8a3(kha): ma ga le༐ ku hu me/a la ke/kal ga/i ri mi/ra d+ha ri/ma ru ga pa ti//rgyal po chen po mig mi bzang gi ming dang stobs dang/dbang phyug dang/byin gyis
8a4(kha): bdag la bde legs su gyur cig swAhA//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis da' tshe 'khor thams cad kyi mdun du seng ge'i sgra bsgrags pa/nga ni stobs bcu dang ldan
8a5(kha): zhing mi 'jigs pa bzhis 'jigs pa med pa ste/'khor gyi nang du seng ge'i sgra khyu mchog lta bu rgya chen po yang dag par sgrag go/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo bskor ro//sde dang
8a6(kha): mthu dang gzhon bcas pa'i//bdud kyang gcig gis rab tu btul//sems can thams cad bsnyung ba'i phyir//rig sngags thams cad nga la nyon//sang yA the dan
8a7(kha): a sang ge/khad ga ba te/ba le nir+g+ho Sha/ra shu re/ra shu re/shu ra d+ha re/badzrang ge ma/badzrang d+ha re/stam b+he Di/Da swa re/bi ra dze ba g+ha se/ba ra gru pra ba te/i ra Na
8b1(kha): d+harma yug de/di sha bi g+ha sh+Te//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan dang/stobs dang dbang phyug dang/byin gyis bdag bde legs su gyur cig swAhA/phyogs bzhi'i 'jig
8b2(kha): rten skyong ba dag/sangs rgyas kyi ni gsung thos nas//'jigs dang skrag dang dngangs gyur te//thal mo sbyar nas 'khod par 'gyur//'byung pa'i tshogs ni lhags pa
8b3(kha): rnams//dngangs 'khrugs rod rod por gyur nas//phyogs bcur rab tu 'byir byed cing//sgra bo che yang sgrogs par byed//rgyal po chen pos de shes nas//gsang ba gsu
8b4(kha): ni yang dag smra//kye ma rig sngags rig sngags che//stong chen rab tu 'joms byed pa//sangs rgyas kyis ni gsungs pa de//thos nas 'byung po skrag gyur te//dper na rab tu
8b5(kha): 'bar me dang//til mar ngar btags ral gri bzhin//ko'u ta mas rab bshad pa'i//rig sngags spu gri'i so dang 'dra//drang srong tshig ni legs bshad pa//de la gang zhig
8b6(kha): ma dad na//de yi bu yi thu bo ni//tshangs pa'i zhags pas med par 'gyur//ma ni rab tu 'bar byas la//bla dang 'og dang phyogs bzhir yang//sbyin bsreg gi ni chus gtor
8b7(kha): te//nas yungs dkar blangs nas su//mar gyi snying po dang sbyar nas//me yi nang du blugs par bya//legs par gsungs pa 'di thos nas//gal te myur du mi gtong na//
9a1(kha): @#//thams cad 'bar bar 'gyur pa ni//mar dang yungs dkar mar blugs bzhin//gnod sbyin chad pas bsngags nas su//de dag bde ba mi rnyed de//de yi rtsib
9a2(kha): legs g.yas par ni//'bras chan dag kyang 'byung bar 'gyur//gnod sbyin nad kyis btab pas na//shra bkra'i nad kyis btab par 'gyur//lcang lo can gyi pho brang du//
9a3(kha): gzhan yang 'gro bar mi 'gyur te//lus ngan grags dang ldan pa yi//khyim yang mthong bar mi 'gyur te//'byung po mang po tshogs pa na//stan yang thob par mi 'gyuro//
9a4(kha): gra nod sbyin dag gi dkyil 'khor du//zas skom dang yang bral bar 'gyur//stong chen rab 'joms mdo sde la//gnod sbyin gang dag mi phyogs pa//de la rdo rje 'chang khros te//
9a5(kha): spyi bo dag ni 'gems par 'gyur//spu gri'i so ni rtsub mo yis//de yi lce ni gcod par 'gyur//mtshon cha shin du rnon po yis//de yi sna dang rna ba gcod//'khor lo spu gri'i
9a6(kha): sa 'dra bas//klad pa rab tu gshog par byed//tho ba lcags kyis bkris pa yis//de yi snying la 'joms par byed//rnag dang khrag ni dron mo dag/de yi kha nas 'dzag par
9a7(kha): 'gyur//rig sngags zhags pa'i chad pa yis//rtag tu 'khor bar 'gro ba na//'khor ba gnod sbyin dkyil 'khor du//de nyid du ni 'khor bar 'gyur//rgyal po chen po dpal
9b1(kha): ldan pa࿒ phyogs bzhi dag tu song gyur nas//chos kyi go cha bgos gyur nas//bzang po'i khri la 'dug pa ni//shar phyogs na ni yul 'khor bsrung //lho phyogs na ni 'phag+sa
9b2(kha): skyes po࿒ ༐nub phyogs na ni mig mi bzad//byang phyogs na ni lus ngan po//dpal dang gzi brjid 'bar ba yi//rgyal po dag ni phyin pa dang//de tshe ston pa thams cad mkhyen 
9b3(kha): par sang gnas la rab tu 'phags//tshangs pas sprul pa'i gzhal med khang//de nas rdo rje'i gdan la bzhugs//de nas tshangs pa tshangs chen rnams//thal mo sbyar khyod
9b4(kha): phyag 'tshal lo//dpal ldan gser gyi ri bo 'dra//gser gyi mchod sdong dang yang mtshungs//me tog pad ma bzhin du rgyas//sa la'i rgyal po bzhin du rgyas//nyi ma dang 
9b5(kha): 'dra zla nya la//skar ma rnams kyis yongs bskor bzhin//thub sku gser gyi kha dog la//mtshan rnams kyis ni yongs su bskor//'jig rten sgron ma bstod byas te//
9b6(kha): 'jig rten mgon gyi spyan sngar ni//'jig rten skyong la 'di skad smras//'jig rten skyong gi 'khor rnams kyis//rjes su bstan+sa pa ma thob ste//gang phyir 'di la sangs rgyas
9b7(kha): dang࿒ rang sangs rgyas rnams 'byung 'gyur zhing//'di las nyan thos rnams kyang skye//lha rnams dag kyang skye bar 'gyur//rig byed yan lag drug mthar phyin//bram ze rnaMs
10a1(kha): @#//kye na skye bar 'gyur//dang srong bskal pa chan pa dang//dge sbyong bram ze 'di las skyes//khyad cag snying las chung byed pa//mi yi skye dgu gnod par 'gyur//
10a2(kha): tshangs pa yi ni ci ka thos nas//'jig rten skyong gis tshig smras pa//tshangs pa chen po de bzhin no//thub pa chen po de bzhin no//bdag gis ri rab bskul byas shing//sa
10a3(kha): yang rnam par bzlog byas la//rgya mtsho la ni thug pa rnams//rnam par sbyang ba nyid du bya//zhags pa dam po ma nyams pa//nyi ma zla ba rlung rnams dang//skar ma dag ni thams
10a4(kha): cad kyang//bdag gis su ni bcing bar bya//gang dag rab tu sdang byed cing//'jig rten la ni phan mi byed//de dag la ni thams cad du//phyogs kyang snang bar mi bya'o//'jig
10a5(kha): rten lha dang bcas pa de//'byung po rnams kyi phyir smod de//'byung po mi yi skye dgu la//gnod par byed cing 'tshe bar byed//gang dag sngags dang sman rnams dang//rig sngags
10a6(kha): las ni 'da' byed pa//'di la sngags kyi dam pa ni//pha rol gnon pa ston par byed//rig sngags las ni 'da' ba'i 'khor//kun du chad pas sdigs byas te//'jig rten
10a7(kha): mgon po'i spyan snga ru//yang dag par yang mchod par bgyi//gang gi 'byung po'i dkyil 'khor kun//'di bshad pa na mi nyan pa//de dag chad pa bshad bya ba//tshangs pas sprul
10b1(kha): pa'i mdo sde'o//sangs rgyas zhabs la phyag 'tshal te//phan tshun du ni blta byas na//bI dur+ya dang gser dngul dang //mu tig rnams dang shel dag dang //slug dang rdo yi snying po dang //
10b2(kha): rin chen bdun gyis brgyan pa yi//rtsibs stong kha dog gser 'dra ba'i//shing rta rnam pa bzhi byas pa//bzo rnams kun dang ldan pa ste//rdzu 'phrul gyis ni mkha' la 'gro//rgyal
10b3(kha): po dag ni der zhugs nas//'byung po'i dkyil 'khor nyid du dong //sa ni gser gyi phye ma dang //me tog dag gis bkang byas nas//mgul par ltung dang bskud pa dang //zhags pa
10b4(kha): dag kyang gdags byos la//phyogs kyi sa ni ji snyed pa'i//'byung po thams cad khrid shog ces//gnod sbyin gyi ni sde dpon kun//phyogs bzhi dag tu bkye bar byas//stong chen rab tu 
10b5(kha): 'joms pa yi//mdo sde yi ni dam pa 'di//tshangs pa'i 'jig rten bar du 'byung //lha rnams kun gyis rnam par bsams//stong chen po yi lus kyis su//gnod sbyin dang ni srin po
10b6(kha): btul//lus ngan po yi tshig thos nas//gnod sbyin gyi ni sde 'don dang //phyogs bzhi dag tu dong nas su//gsang ba po la drag tu bsgo//thub pa 'byung po nyid du brgyad//dri za las byung 
10b7(kha): gdon gyi bdag/shar phyogs chen 'khod pa dag/mdo sde'i zhags pas de dag kun//bcing ba lnga yis bsdams pa yi//'byung po mang po nga la nyon//thub pa 'byung po nyi shu 
11a1(kha): @#//brgyad po grul bum las byung ba don gyi rnams//lho phyogs cha 'dir 'khod pa dag/mdo sde'i zhags pas de dag kun//bcu ba lnga yis bsdams pa yi//
11a2(kha): 'byung po mang po nga la nyon//thub pa 'byung po nyi shu brgyad//klu las gdon du gyur pa rnams//nub phyogs cha 'dir 'khod pa ngag/mdo sde'i zhags pas re dag kun
11a3(kha): bcing ba lnga yis bsdams pa yi//'byung po mang po nga la nyo na//thab pa 'byung po nyi shu ba rgyad//gnod sbyin las ni gdon gyur pa//byang phyogs cha 'dir 'khod par dag/
11a4(kha): mdo sde'i zhags pas de dang kun//bcing ba lnga yis bsdams ba yi//'byung po mang po nga la nyan//lus ngan po yi bu thu bo//kun du rgyal po mi la zhan//de yi drung na 'khod
11a5(kha): pa yi//gnod sbyin bye ba drug bcu dag/bcing ba rnam pa lngas bsdams nas//mdo sde'i zhags pas kun du drangs//de nyid kyi ni bu gnyis pa//skyes pa po zhes rnams grags pa
11a6(kha): de yi drung na 'khod pa yi//gnod sbyin bye pa drug bcu rnams//bcing ba rnam pa lngas pa lngas bsdams nas/mdo sde'i zhags pas kun tu drangs//de nyid kyi ni ba gsum pa//de yi ming
11a7(kha): ni gdon po che//de yi drung na 'khod pa ya//gnod sbyin bye ba drug cu rnams//bcing ba rnams pa lngas bsdams nas//bsod sde'i zhags pas kun du drings//de nyid kyi ni bu bzhi pa//
11b1(kha): de yi ming ni bum pa'i lte//de yi phung na 'khod pa yi//gnod sbyin bye ba drug bcu rnams//bcing ba rnam pa lngas bsdams nas//mdo snga'i zhags pas kun du drangs//dbang phyug
11b2(kha): chen po lha chen po//lag bzhi pa la stobs po che//de yi drung na 'khod pa yi//gnong sbyin bya ba drug bcu rnams//bci ba rnam pa lngas bsdams na//mdo snga'i zhags pas
11b3(kha): kun du drangs//'byung po 'joms pa'i ri la ni//'byung po thams cad lhags nas su//rig sngags thams cad 'byung ba'i gnas//rig sngags 'di ni kun du bsams//chad pa
11b4(kha): mi bzad drag po ste//gang phyir sangs rgyas kun gyis gsungs//ko'u tam la gus par ni//thams cad 'di la skyabs su 'dod//g.yo med par ni rjes bzhugs shing //thams cad
11b5(kha): med par gyur te re//de nas thang cig tsam gyis ni//'byung po mang po thams cad lhags//ri rnams dang ni g.yang sa dang //rgya mtsho dang ni mtsho rnams dang //klung dang 'bab chu lu ma
11b6(kha): dang //klong dka na gang 'khod dang //skyed mos tshal dang gzhal med khang //kun dga' dang ni nags tshal dang //mchod rten gnas dang grong gi gnas//shing ljan drung na gang gnas
11b7(kha): dang //grong khyer sgo dang gong dang ni//gong brdal dang ni ljongs rnams dang //rgyal po'i pho brang sgo rnams dang //gzhal med khang na gang gnas dang //dkyil 'khor dag dang dur khrod dang
12a1(kha): @#//de bzhin lha khang rnams dang ni//'tshams dang sho gam gnas rnams dang //khang steng dang ni gram sa dang //gnod sbyin gang rnams bla 'og dang //phyogs bzhi
12a2(kha): dang ni 'tshams rnams nas//gnod sbyin skye ba stong rnams ni//rig sngags zhags pas bkug pa rnams//rdza rnga dag ni brdungs nas su//la las lnga zlu ma dag ni brdungs//pi
12a3(kha): wang gling bu mkhar ba'i rnga//stobs chen rnams ni sgra 'byin te//rol mo'i dbyangs sgra sna tshogs dang //glu dang gar rnams byas nas su//dbang po zla ba chu lha dang //bar dwa dza skye 
12a4(kha): dgu'i bdag/ma ldan pa dang kun du blta//mngon shes gangs can rdzogs pa dang //tsan dan dang ni 'dod mchog dang //mgul pa mdzes dang mgul nges dang //skye dgu'i bla mar 
12a5(kha): gyur pa dang //lha las byung ba'i ma ldan dang //dri za sna tshogs sde dang ni//mi rgyal rgyal ba khyu mchog dang //de bzhin phud bu lnga zhes bya//g.yer ma dang ni nyi zer dang //nya lji bas dang 
12a6(kha): ni ral gyi bu//kun gyi bshes dang grags 'dzin dang //'brog gnas dang ni yid bzangs dang //khab rna dang ni ri sul dang //lnga 'dzin dang ni khab bzangs dag/sde dang dbu'i dang bzhi 
12a7(kha): par bcas//lha dang klu dang dri za dang //gnod sbyin srin po lha ma yin//nyin gsum pa yi smyo byed dang //de bzhin bzhi pa'i rigs dag dang //'jig rten 'tshe ba gang yin pa//gnod 
12b1(kha): sbyin srin po ma rungs rnams//phyogs bzhi nas ni bkug nas su//bcing ba lnga yis bsdams pa rnams//'jig rten mgon po'i sbyin snga ru//thal sbyar 'khod nas 'di skad smras/
12b2(kha): phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpal khyod la//phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la//thal mo sbyang nas phyag 'tshal lo//chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//de dag gi ni mdun rgyug pa//'byung
12b3(kha): po gnod sbyin lus po che//lag pa bzhi pa ma rungs pa//rkang mangs dang ni rkang gcig pa//rkang bzhi pa dang rkang gnyis dang //rkang pa sgreng ba'i spyi btsug dang //lus mang mig gi
12b4(kha): gcig pa dang //lus gcig pa la mgo bzhi dang //mig mangs po la lus phyed dang //mig gcig lto ni bcu gnyis dang //bung bu rnga mo glang chen mgo/lag pa bsgreng la migo btud dang 
12b5(kha): mtshon cha'i so dang mtshon cha'i lag/mtshon cha'i rkang pa srin po rnams//zangs kyi skra dang zangs kyi so//zangs kyi mig dang zangs kyi lag/zangs kyi tho ba rkang pa dang //zangs kyi sna
12b6(kha): dang lcags kyi kha//rkang lag kun du 'bar ba dang //mi yi lus su gnas pa rnams//sgur zhar lba ba gsum yod par//mig mi stug la kha ral po//chu srin stag gi lcags
12b7(kha): dang ni//gzugs mi stug la kha ral po//mchu 'phyangs po dang so yon po//rab tu gtum po khro gnyer can//gsus po che dang bum rna bo//rna 'phyangs po dang rna ba med//lag rings
13a1(kha): @#//
13b1(kha): phyogs phyogs dag tu rnam par rgyug/grong khyer rnams kyi sgo dag dang//khyim nas khyim du rnam par 'ther//rlung dang bad kan mkhris pa rnams//phyogs bzhi dag tu 'khrugs par byed/
13b2(kha): de ltar don rgyal po yi//gdon dang 'jigs pa chen po gnas//lhags pa de dag thams cad kyang//rig sngags zhags pas rab tu bsdams//phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpa' khyod la//
13b3(kha): phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyed la//thal mo sbyar nas phyag 'tshal lo//chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//grong khyer grong dang yul 'khor dang//pho brang 'khor du rgyu ba ni//gnod
13b4(kha): spyi ni ma rungs mdangs 'phrog pa//snying rje med cing khrag 'thung ba//lus chen 'jigs pa chen po ste//stobs pa che la sgra yang che//mgrin bcu po la mig stong po//gdon chen mig
13b5(kha): ni dmar po rnams//'khor ni mang po rnams btang ste//lag na mtsho ni thogs rab tu 'jigs//sbrul gyi lpags pa thogs pa dang//lag bzhi lag na sgron mar thogs//lag na mtshan
13b6(kha): dang dbyug pa thogs//rdo rjes dung rtse lag thogs pas//rab tu drag pa'i dmag rgal nas//phyogs kun du ni skrag par byed//sdang ba'i gnas na 'khor pa yi//gsang ba po ni dad
13b7(kha): mo kun gyi//mi yi 'jig rten 'di dag tu//skyes pa bud med gzhan yang 'dzin//sha khrag dron mor bcas pa la//rnam par 'khrug cing 'dod pa'i gzugs//seng ge dang ni stag
14a1(kha): @#//dang rta Ima ha ba gang bung bu rma//glang po che'i gzugs 'dra dang //dom dang gzig dang spyang khyi dang //khyi dang lpa dang ri dgas dang //byi la byi ba spre'u'i gzugs//
14a2(kha): bse dang phag dang spra mor dang //nya dang rus sbal dag gri gzugs//kha cig dag ni sram gyi gzugs//khwa dang 'ug pa khu byug dang //bya dang rged khra dang nya med bcas//rma bya 
14a3(kha): dang pa thi ba dang //khrung khrung dang ni bya rnams dang //kha cig bya gag gzugs rnams su//gnod sbyin rnams ni mi yis mthong //'dab bzangs bya yi tshul gyis kyang //skye bo mang
14a4(kha): po skrag par byed//khi cig mi yi mgo can la//lus ni bya gag bung bu 'dra//kha cig mtshon gyis rab tu gces//lus dang bral ba dag tu snang //rnam par 'khrugs shing 'dod
14a5(kha): la myos//rgyu ma'i phreng bas kun du bkris//rtse gsum mdung gis rab 'debs shing //srog chags rnams la gnod par byed//mi zad pa yi dbugs 'byin cing //skye dgu 'di  
14a6(kha): dag skrag par byed//srog chags rgyud ni ji snyed par//sna tshogs kyi ni gzugs su seng //kha cig dag ni ri yang thogs//la la ral gri 'khor lo thogs//grangs ni brgya
14a7(kha): phrag stong rnams ste//dbyug pas bsngags shing kha drag po//mig phyung ba la bzhin mi stug/srin po so ni rno ba dang //sna dang rna ba bcad pa dang //lce bcad gdong cig 
14b1(kha): ma can//rkang lag dgra ni brtubs pa dang //mgo bo bcad pa'i srin po rnams//skabs ni rab tu tshol bar byed//sdig pa can rnams gzi brjid 'phrog/mi dag gi ni lus rnams 
14b2(kha): la//rab tu phra ba'i gdon du snang //spu yi bar dang nad rnams dang //de bzhin rma yi kha rnams su//lhags pa de dag thams cad dang //rig sngags zhags pas rab tu drangs//phyag 'tshal 
14b3(kha): skyas bu dpa' khyod la//phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la//thal mo sbyar nas phyag 'tshal lo//chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//ri yi rgyal po ri rab dang //de bzhin kha ra khor 
14b4(kha): yug dang //byo rgod phung po shol mda' 'dzin//spos 'di ldan pa gangs kyi ri//sna tshogs rtsegs dang ba Da pa//na la ta yo ri gnyis dang //dpal gyi ri yi rtse mo dang //gang dag shin 
14b5(kha): du 'thor 'phags dang //ri bo de dag kun la yang //lha dang drang srong gang gnas pa//de dag thams cad mi dga' zhing //'jigs pa'i yid kyis mya ngan byed//bye ba stong gi lha dag 
14b6(kha): dang //bye ba phrag brgya'i lha rnams dang //lha yi bu mo skal chen dgo//thal mo rab tu sbyar bar byed//thag bzangs ris dang sgra gcan dang //sim par byed pa'ang der phyin te//lha min bye ba 
14b7(kha): phag sdong dang //lha min bye ba phyag bcad dang //de dag du mye rdzu 'phrul can//mang po kun kyang thal ma sbyor//rab tu gnas dang rgya mtsho dang //klu yi rgyal po gzi can dang //klu yi rgyal 
15a1(kha): @#//pa ma ngas bde gda' bo 'di dga' gnyis po dang//rdo rje blo gros phag shu dang//gang ga dga' bo sin du mtsho//bya yi rgyal po 'dab bzangs dang//rgya mtsho chen po
15a2(kha): 'khrug par byed//stong phrag bya ba'i klu rnams dang//bye ba brgya yi klu dag dang//de dag bu mo skal chen rnams//thal mo rab tu sbyar bar byed//nyi ma zla ba gnyis ka yang//skar
15a3(kha): ma'i 'khor gyis yongs su bskor//gser gyi me tog yan lag na//ma ga ta na 'jigs byed pa//gso ba'i mtha' na mi thod can//ko se la na rab tu dkar//bzang po'i yul na kha
15a4(kha): spu can//gyad kyi yul na grags pa 'dzin//lnga 'dzin pa na skrag par byed//rta skyes pa na dmagi dmar gnas//srung byed pa na ser skya gnas//sems pa na ni mig ser po//be'u
15a5(kha): la na bum pa'i lto//dga' ba'i yul na ring po skyes//ba glang 'dzin na rab tu 'joms//dung yul na ni nyi ma'i grogs//ljongs chen rnams kyi gnod sbyin ni//brjod pa bcu drug de
15a6(kha): dag dang lag na rdo rje gnod sbyin che//chos skyong rab tu dkar ba dang//ser skya rab mdzes khyab 'jug dang//bum lto gsus pa zlum po dang//chu srin bden pa 'chang ba dang//lnga ldan
15a7(kha): rgyal ba khyu mchog dang//gnod sbyin chen po dbang phyug che//lag pa bzhi can stobs po che//sde dpa' rab tu 'dul ba ni//mthu bo che la stobs po che//gshin rje gshin rje'i pho nya dang/
15b1(kha): bdud rnams sde dang bcas pa dang //gnod sbyin bye ba'i g.yog 'chang ba//gnod sbyin de ming 'phrog pa yin//sha ri ta ni sder bcas dang //ri yi bu mo gnod sbyin ma//gzi brjid chen 
15b2(kha): mo 'jigs pa'i gzugs//'phrog ma zhes kyang grags pa dang //gdol pa mo ni gtum mo dang //bu rnams lnga brgyas bskor ba dang //mi bdud nag mo karka ti//pad ma dang ni phad 
15b3(kha): ma bcas//me tog so dangs ga deng //srin mo bong bu rna can dang //tsan dan dang ni khyab 'jug+sa len//seng ge ser skya dmar ser dang //glang po klu yi so can dang //ri bshes drag po 
15b4(kha): mche ba can//srin mo so bzang yod pa dang //tshangs pa len+sa dang khyab 'jug len//gnod sbyin ha la ha la dang //gnod sbyin rnam pa'i mchu can dang //mdung dang mda' gzhu kun thog+sa 
15b5(kha): shing /mi rta ba glang ri dgas rnams//za zhing 'tshe ba de dag ni//phyogs rnams kun du rgyug cing dong //sa 'di rab tu g.yo bar byed//nags tshal rnams kyang rab tu skyems//ro rnams 
15b6(kha): kun nas g.yo bar byed//skye dgu 'di dag 'tshe byed cing //phan tshun nas ni 'du ba dag/rig sngags kyis ni grangs nas lags//phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpa' khyod la//phyag 'tshal 
15b7(kha): skyes bu mchog khyod la//thal mo steng nas phyag 'tshal lo//chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//'jig rten mgon po'i spyan snga ru//de dag lags par gyur pa dang //de nas rgyal 
16a1(kha): @#//pho rdas thas bdag 'jig rten mgon la 'di skad gsol//bdag gi phyogs cha byang logs su//rgyal po'i gnas su sprul pa ni//lcang le ldan pas yid du 
16a2(kha): mchid pa de bas bdag ni lung le'i bdag/rgyal po'i pho brang lcang lo can//lha rnams kun gyis bsams par gyur//phyi ra shan du mthon phe can//rin chen kun gyis brgyan
16a3(kha): pa lags//phang du dpag tshad bcu drug ste//gser gyi rgyu las bgyis pa lags//phyogs bzhi'i cog ni re re lags//gnod sbyin rdo rje 'chang ba yang //kun nas rab tu gnas
16a4(kha): par 'gyur//lag pa gser can legs par sprul//rgyal po'i pho brang de yi sgo//bzhi po gtod pa bgyis pa ni//gcig ni gser las bgyis pa'i sgo//gnyis ni dngul
16a5(kha): las bgyis pa ste//gsum pa shel gyi sgo mo lags//bzhi pa gser gyis rnam par spras//grong khyer de yi nang dag na//skyed mos tshal gyi me tog rgyas//de yi nang na gzhal
16a6(kha): med khang //rin chen bdun rgyu sna tshogs mchis//rin chen sna tshogs ljon shing can//bya phran sna tshogs skad kyang 'byin//me tog sna tshogs legs par bkram//sna tshogs spos
16a7(kha): kyis byugs pa legs//gnod sbyin mo ni 'joms ma dang //glu dang rol mos rab tu brgyan//dpal gyi dam pa nyams su myong //der ni 'byung po'i dkyil 'khor du//gang dag 'khar
16b1(kha): zhing mchis pa dag/rnam pa' mchog kun ldan pa ste//chos 'chal ba dang chos spyod pa//srog chags rnams la 'tshe mi bgyid//zas dang skom gyis mi brel te//skye dgu mi zad
16b2(kha): gzhan dag ni//longs spyod chen po tshol bgyid cing//phyogs bzhi kun du rnam par blta//grong khyer sgo yi gam yod na//bza' shing ra ba nags tshal na//gnod sbyin srin po 'byung
16b3(kha): ba'i tshogs//bya ba stong phrag brgya mchis pa//de dag thams cad mdo sde yi//zhags pas drangs te rlag par bgyi//rgyal po'i pho brang dbus na ni//chos kyi rgyal po gnas gyur pa//
16b4(kha): bsil ba'i rdzing bus bskor ba ste//tsan dan nags kyis 'khor ba lags//kun nas dpag tshad bcu drug ste//khang bu brtsegs ma sprul pa dag/gcig ni gser las bgyis pa ste//
16b5(kha): gnyis pa dngul las bgyis pa lags//gsum pa bA du ru la bgyis//bzhi pa tsan dan shel gyi rgyu//lnga pa mu tig dmar po ste//drug pa rdo yi snying po lags//bdun pa spug
16b6(kha): las bgyis pa ste//brgyad pa rin chen bdun las bgyis//bud med stong phrag brgya dag kyang//de dag re re kun na gnas//lha ba lhub sa tshogs mchog dag dang//cha byad
16b7(kha): sa tshogs mang pos brgyan//gnang ral mi sna tshogs dang//bzo yi gnas la mkhas pa mchis//de ltar rab tu dga' ba yis//bde zhing tshim pa'i yid gyur cing//de na bdag
17a1(kha): @#//na dgun skyes dang 'ded pa dag gis rab myos pas//bdag gi 'khor rnams 'byin bgyid cing //phyogs bcu dag tu 'jigs pa bskyed//bud med dag dang skyes
17a2(kha): pa dang /de bzhin khye'u du mo dang //mngal na gnas pa'ang 'jigs par bgyid//dud 'gro'i skye gnas gtogs la'ang 'tshe//ska rngam dag dang nyi zla dang //gtum pa'i gza' 
17a3(kha): rnams gnod par bgyid//lo thog sa bon 'bras bu dang //me tog sman dang zas skom dang //mi rnams kyi ni dpal 'phrog cing //mthon po rnams kyi dma' bar bgyid//'jig
17a4(kha): rten dag na nongs pa rnams//dmag dang 'thab mo sha gnyer dang //tshig gam zhig gam bsad byed pa//de kun 'byung po'i rnam par gyur//sled cing stor bar bgyid pa dang //bzlog
17a5(kha): par bgyid cing dgod par bgyid//'jigs par bgyid cing blta bar bgyid//dgrar gyur pa yang ston par bgyid//sdig pa'i rmi lam mthong bar bgyid//nyal ba dag la gnod 
17a6(kha): par bgyid//sgo nas se gol sri byung zhing //mi snyan brjod de za bar bgyid//mdza' bo gnyen bshes gzugs dag tu//dga' zhing bka' mchid smra bar bgyid//bu mo sdug gu'i 
17a7(kha): gzugs dag tu//'dod la spyod pa bdar bar bgyid//nyi ma skar ma'i gzugs dang ni//zla ba'i gzugs su bda' bar bgyid//rlung dang skar ma lce 'bab gzugs//tshe la
17b1(kha): gnod pa de dag bda'//skar mda'i tshogs dang 'dra ba dag/'jigs par bgyid pa wa dang khyir//rnam par sprul nas ljon shing dang //mchod rten dag gi gnas na bda'//skyes pa 
17b2(kha): gzhon nu'i gzugs mdzes pas//'bar zhing shing rta'i sgra yang 'byin//gnas rnams dang ni shul dag tu//rnam pa sna tshogs rjes kyang ston//grong khyer dag dang khyim rnams kyi//sgo
17b3(kha): yang bden chen de dag 'byed//srog dang lus kyang blang bgyis shing //lam ngan kun du bskor bar bgyid//rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs dag dang //rnam pa sna tshogs sgra rnams dang //rnam
17b4(kha): pa sna tshogs nad kyi rnams//lus la na bar gnas par ston//nad rnams kun gyi mtshan nyid kyang //phyin ci log tu ston par bgyid//ji snyed 'jig rten rang bzhin dag/thams cad
17b5(kha): log par de dag ston//rig sngags zhags pas drangs pas na//de dag thams cad 'dir mchis so//de nas rgyal chen rnam thos bu//langs te thal mo rab sbyar nas//zhabs
17b6(kha): la 'khor lo'i mtshan mnga' ba//gser gyi mchod sdong 'bar ba bzhin//'jig rten snang bar mdzad pa po//thub pa chen po me dang 'dra//gnod sbyin gsang sngags mi bzad can 
17b7(kha): song phrag drug bcu rtsa bzhi dag bang phyogs cha yi phyogs na gnas//byang phyogs dag tu gnod par bgyid//'jig rten mgon gyi spyan sngar ni//de dag chad pa bdag gis bshad//
18a1(kha): @#//skyed ya speng na kha kha khar go//khar gi/pi tsa k+Sha Ne/tsag kre/tsan dre/tsa pa li/b+hi ma par ba te/ba ru gre/ku De ra ke/a ka kI/brga pa ti/sa rang ga pa te

18a7(kha): za stong phrag drug bcu bzhi//shar phyogs cha yi phyogs na gnas//shar phyogs pa la gnod pa bgyid//'jig rten gyi mgon gyi spyan sngar ni//de dag chad pa bdag gis
18b1(kha): bshad//s+ya da ya the dan/d+ha ra ni/d+ha ra ni/pra d+han sa ni/b+han dza ni/pra ban dza ni/b+hi d+ha ma ni/ki ma pu ru Sha/ka sha la/s+tha ra te/shu ra ha re/shud d+ha tsa re Ne/g+ho sha
18b2(kha): pa te;sha ra ge/shan ti//shar phyogs su bdag bde legs su gyur cig sa ha//tshangs pa dang ni brgya byin dang//'jig rten skyong dang dbang phyug che//gnod sbyin sde
18b3(kha): dpon thams cad dang//'phrog ma bu dang bcas pa yang//bdag gi me tog spos dig dang//sbyin bsreg 'do ni bling bar bgyis//de dag gi ni brtson 'grus dang//gzi brjid

18b7(kha): gru lu bum ying btags chu lu lo dog/steng phrag drug bcu rtsa bzhi rnams//lho phyogs cha yi phyogs na gnas//de nas 'byung la gnad par bgyid//'jig rten mgon
19a1(kha): @#//gyi spyan sngar ni//de dag chad pa bdag gis bshad//s+yad ya the dan/shan ti/sha ra ti/ka na ti/ka ra ti/ging ga si/ki dkar ti/ki ma ba Di/
19a2(kha): b+ha ri ni/d+ha ri ni/tsa pa ti/b+hu mi ba Di dri/hi ma pa ti/dz+yo ti dza ra Ni gar gri///lho phyogs su bdag bde legs su gyur cig swAhA/tshangs pa dang ni brgya byin dang

20a1(kha): @#//la gnas bzhi bdon//'jig rten mgon gyi spyan sngar ni//de dag chad pa bdag gis bshad//spyad ya tha dan/pra me pra ma ge//ga ma swa re/badzra badzre g+ha

20b1(kha): rgyal srid gcig ga phyir 'jig rten du byung ba ma yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/grong dang/grong brdal dang/ljongs dang/grong khir dang/khyim nas sems

21b1(kha): ldan khangs ngag slong gi dge 'dun dang thabs gcig tu bya rgod phung po'i ra las shod du babs te/yangs pa can gyi gong khyer chen po ga la ba der gshegs pa dang/yangs pa can
21b2(kha): gyi la ce bya rnams kyis/bcom ldan 'das shin du mdzes pa dang ba 'os pa/dbang po zhi ba/thugs zhi ba/dbang po dul ba/thugs dul ba/dul ba dang zhi gnas kyi dam par
21b3(kha): pha rol du phyin pa brnyes pa/dbang po dben zhing khugs pa/gang po che ltar shin du dul ba/mtsho ltar dangs zhing rog pa med pa/rnam par gsal ba/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan
21b4(kha): sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis sku legs par brgyan pa/dpe byad bzang po brgyad bcus shin du brgyan pa/shing sa la'i rgyal po ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnam par spras pa/

21b7(kha): bcom ldan 'das yangs pa can gyi gong khyer chen por lam gang nas gshegs pa'i lam de phyag cing/chag chag 'debs/byi dor byed/me tog 'thar/dar gyi lda ldi dang
22a1(kha): @#//dril ba 'am gzigs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan sna tshogs kyis rgyan te/bdug spos sna tshogs kyis bdugs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba dang dong 
22a2(kha): ste phyin pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis phyag gi mthil 'khor lo rtsibs stong dang ldan pa'i ri mo 
22a3(kha): can|me tog pad ma'i snying po ltar 'jam pa sngon legs par spyad pa'i mtshan gyis brgyan pa/gzhon sha chags shing 'jam pa nyi ma'i 'od zer bas lhag pa'i 'od dang 
22a4(kha): ldan pa|'od zer brgya phag mang po kan du 'phel/dri ma med pa'i phyag gis li tsa byi de dag gi mgo la byugs te/rjes su bstan pa mdzad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das

29b7(kha): brgya byin gyi gzugs sam/rgyal po chen po bzhi'i gzugs khri'u 'am za ma tog dang bzhag ste/phyag brgya bgyis la bzhag par bgyi'o//me tog sna tshogs dang/spos sna
30a1(kha): @#//tshogs dang 'byongs pa dang /brgya byin dang /rgyal po chen po bzhi dang /dbang phyug chen po dang /gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon dang /gnod sbyin stobs po che dang /'phrog 
30a2(kha): ma la sogs pa yang gis dkon mchog gsum la phyag bgyi'o//de rnams kyi stobs dang /dbang phyug dang /byin gyis bdag la bde legs su gyur cig/bdag la bsrung ba 
30a3(kha): gyis shig/nad thams cad las thar bar gyur cig/nad pa'i zas dang /skom dang /sman rnams dang /rig sngags 'dis btab ste bstsal bar bgyi'o//rdzogs sangs rnam 
30a4(kha): par sangs rgyas tshe//'jig rten skyong bas phyogs bzhi nas//snod bzhi dag ni blangs nas su//bde bar gshegs la phul ba dang //mthu yis gcig tu sprul mdzad pa 
30a5(kha): lha yi snod mchog de dang ni//bdud rtsi 'dra ba'i sman gyi rnams//ston pa'i phyag na bsnams gyur pa//bden pa'i tshig ni de dag gis//sman rnams bdud rtsir 'gyur bar 
30a6(kha): shog/de bzhin lha mo 'phrog ma yi//lha rjes dge ba a ka ru//blangs nas ston la rab du lo ba//bdud rtsi 'dra ba'i smanagyi rnams//bden pa'i tshig ni de bdag gis//nad 
30a7(kha): pa rnams kyi nad sol cing //nyes pa thams cad 'phrog byed pa//sman rnams bdud rtsir gyur bar shog/sangs rgyas rnam gzigs gzi brjid dang /gtsug tor can 
30b1(kha): gyi stobs rnams dang //thams cad skyob kyi bden tshig dang //log dad sel gyi ting 'dzin dang //gser zhes bya ba'i ye shes dang //'od bsrung gi ni rdzu 'phrul dang //shAkya
30b2(kha): seng ge'i mthu rnams kyis//sman rnams bdud rtsir 'gyur bar shog/nad pa shar phyogs kha bstan la//sman rnams nye bar bstsal par bgyi//de yi tshe ni rig sngags 'di//
30b3(kha): lag mthil bzhag bklag par bgyi//sad yA the dan/kha te kha te/kha Ta bi kha Te/tsa le byi tsa le/bi lam bA/ba le ba la ba te/tsan te/tsa ra Ne/a mri ta Ner g+ho She swAhA//
30b4(kha): rlung las gyur dang mkhris las gyur//bod kan las gyur 'dus las gyur//nad rnams thams cad rab bcom ste//bdag la rtag tu bde legs shog/byad dang ri langs dang
30b5(kha): ldan pa'i las rnams la skyes pa 'am/bud med gdugs zhag gcig stung ba bgyis pa rab tu bkrus pa shin du rgyan pa/des dog sa la me tog sil ma bkram ste/spas
30b6(kha): sna tshogs kyis bdags la//seng leng dang rgya shug rigs gnyis kyi zhugs bus te/sa bon thams cad phyogs bzhir gtor bar bgyi/zhugs kyi nang du yang gtor bar
30b7(kha): bgyi'o/rtsa ba thams cad dang me tog thams cad tshon skud sna tshogs la brgyus te/ral gri 'am mdung rtse gsum pa 'am/mdung ngam/mda' la gdags par bgyi'o//
31a1(kha): @#//spos sna tshogs sbyar te bum pa chen po'i nang du blugs la/gang byed kyis phig pa de skud pa des bcings te/bum pa'i tshab kyis bku bar bgyi'o//skud pa 
31a2(kha): yang mtshon gyis bcad de zhugs kyi nang du dor ro//stong chen po rab tu 'joms pa'i mdo 'di yang brjod par bgyi'o//gang dag sangs rgyas rang sangs rgyas//sangs rgyas 
31a3(kha): rnams kyi nyan thos dang //tshangs dbang dang ni 'jig rten skyong //gnod sbyin sde dpon dbang phyug dang //de bzhin gnod sbyin stobs chen dang //'phrog+sa ma bu dang bcas pa dang //
31a4(kha): de dag mthu dang gzi brjid kyis//ro langs las rnams chad gyur cig/rdo rjes rin chen phug pa dang //me yis bud shing bsreg pa dang //rlung gis sprin ni 'thor ba dang //nyi mas
31a5(kha): nags tshal skyems byid pa//bden pa'i tshig ni de dag gis//byang sdems las rnams tshig par shog/spos dang me tog sna tshogs kyis//sdig pa thams cad nges par
31a6(kha): bsal//tad yA tha/hu me hu me/ka kha li/ka kha li/kha ra li/kha dzu hu ni/dzu ba li/ka ra be/ha ra ri ni/bag pa ri/shan ti/pra shan ti swAhA/d+ha ba ni swAhA/
31a7(kha): gan d+har be swAhA/bya lang ga na swAhA/sarba ka khor nga /tshe nga ni swAhA//lha thams cad kyi gsang sngags 'di dag gis bdag gi byang dang /ro langs dang /sman dang sogs
31b1(kha): dang dug dang dug sbyar ma rnams bcad do//bshig go pham par byas so//pha rol pham par byas so swahA/lba ba dang/shu ba dang//smyo ba dang/'bras dang/'phrul bu
31b2(kha): dang rkam po dang/dug 'thungs pa las thar par 'tshal ba shin du khrus bgyis pa shin du rgyan pa/stan bzang po'i steng du mi chas te/rig sngags 'di brjod par bgyi'o//
31b3(kha): sangs rgyas kun gyi gzi brjid dang//rang rgyal dag gi gzi brjid dang//dgra bcom rnams kyi mthu dag dang//gsang sngags 'dzin pa thams cad dang//shA ri'i bu yi
31b4(kha): shes rab dang//me'u 'gal gyi bu'i rdzu 'phrul dang//ma 'gags pa yi mig dang ni//'od srungs sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang//ko'u di n+ya ya sngar thos dang//kun dga'i yi thos
31b5(kha): pa dang/tshangs pa yi ni byams pa dang//brgya byin gyi ni dbang rnams dang//'jig rten skyong ba'i yul rnams dang//dbang phyug chen po'i stobs dag dang//bde dpon rnams kyi
31b6(kha): mthu dag dang/'phrog ma'i gzi brjid rdzu 'phrul dang//de dag rnams kyi mthu dang gzis//bdag gi dug ni dug med shog/dug med dug rnams 'jig byed pa//gsang sngags
31b7(kha): tshig rnams bde lags so//sang ya the dan/hi ri ka shi/ta ki li/i hi ra/a ma ri/ban Da ra/ke Da De/ke yu'u she ha si kha si/si si/kha ri da ga/ga ru Na swAhA/
32a1(kha): @#//I I I I I de swAhA/me li swAhA//'bras dang rma dang shu ba dang //g.yan pa dang ni 'phrum bu dang //lkog pa dang ni g.ya' ba dang //bdun pa lags te rab
32a2(kha): tu bcom//I I I I I I I de dag 'jig rten dug gsum ste//bcom ldan sangs rgyas dug mi mnga'//sangs rgyas I I yas dug bcom mo//'dod
32a3(kha): chags zhe sdang gti mug rnams//de dag 'jig rten dug gsu ma ste//bcom ldan chos ni dug mi mnga'//chos kyi gzi yis dug bcom ma//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rnaMs
32a4(kha): de dag 'jig rten dug gsung ste//bcom ldan dge 'dun dug mi mnga'//dge 'dun gzi yis dug bcom mo//sa ni dug gi ma yin te//sa ni dug gi pha yang yin//bden pa'i
32a5(kha): bden tshig de dag gis//bdag gi dug ni dug med shog/dug ni sa yi nang du song //gang ba'i snod du dug song shig swAhA//de nas khrag 'khrug pa dang /'thab pa dang /rtsod pa
32a6(kha): dang 'ged pa dang /pha rol gyi dmag tshogs dang /dgra rnams las rgyal bar 'tshal bas/thog mar mchod rten chen po la mchod par bgyi'o//rig sngags kyi rgyal mo steng
32a7(kha): rten mo rab tu 'joms pa 'di yang brjod par bgyi/sangs rgyas kyis ni bdud pham mdzad//chos ma yin ni chos kyis so//dge 'dun gyis ni mu stegs pham//dbang
32b1(kha): pos lha min pham par gyur//lha min rnams kyis zla ba I//nam mkha' lding gis rgya mtsho'o//mes ni shing rnams pham par gyur//chus ni me yang pham par gyur//
32b2(kha): rlung gis sprin rnams rab tu pham//rdo rjes yis ni rin chen phug/lha rnams bde bar rab tu gnas//sa yang bden par gnas pa lags//sangs rgyas dang ni ches kyang
32b3(kha): bden//bden par rgyal gyur rdzun ma lags//sad yA tha dan/a mri te/a ga pu shwe/bi bud pu le/ni ba ra ni/swA r+tha a rta sa d+ha ni/a pa ra dzi te/ba ra ba ra ni/gu h+ya ba te/
32b4(kha): go'u ta me/gu gu ta me/dzam b+ha ni swA hA/staM b+ha ni swA hA/pra b+han dza ni swA hA/ba la pra ban dza ni swA hA/dza ye swA hA/ba dza ye swA hA/dza ye bi dza ye swA hA//phyir rgol ba ni kun las
32b5(kha): rgyal;sdig pa thams cad rab tu pham swA hA//de nas ston pa kun mkhyin gyis//tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs//mi 'khrugs pa dang snang mdzad spyan ras gzigs
32b6(kha): kyi dbang//rin chen 'od 'phro ri rab mu khyud 'od dpag med//rdo rje rnams kyi mtshan rnams rtag tu bzung na ni//'jigs par 'gyur ba ma yin gnod par mi 'gyur ro//gang gis
32b7(kha): mtha stobs chen po brgyad po 'di dag gi/mtshan rnams rjes su bzung ba'i phyir ni rab brjod cing//kun du skyab pa byas nas de yi lus la ni//me dang dug dang mtshon gyis tshug
33a1(kha): @#//par mi 'gyur ro//gal te de la gnod par byed pa nye bar gnas//gshed mas mtshon rnams kyis ni gdab par gzas pa las//sbyin ras gzigs kyi
33a2(kha): dbang po rjes su dran na ni//mtshon cha de dag dum bu dum bur chag cing 'brul//gal te mtshon cha rnams ni thogs par gyur na yang//lag pa chag nas sa la rab tu ltung bar
33a3(kha): 'gyur ba de yi lus la cung zad 'bab par mi 'gyur te//sngan chad byas pa gang yin pa ni ma gtogs so//lha rnams kyis 'thun par tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsol
33a4(kha): te|sangs rgyas spyod yul mtha' yas khyod la phyag 'tshal lo//bden pa rab ston thub pa khyod la phyag 'tshal lo //deng ni bden pa rnams la gnas nas mchis par bgyi
33a5(kha): bdag ni lus rnams thams cad 'bras bur bcas gyur cig/de nas tshangs pa tshangs chen gyis//langs te thal mo sbyar btud nas//stong chen rab tu 'joms byed pa//lag
33a6(kha): par gsungs pa'i rigasngags 'di//byis pa rnams la phan bgyid pa//rigasngags bdag gis brjod par bgyi//gang gis dang por 'dzam gling du//rigasngags rab tu ston
33a7(kha): mdzad pa//chos kyi rgyal po snang mdzad pa//sangs rgyas dpal la phyag 'tshal lo//khyad par 'phags pa'i chos rnams dang//tshogs kyi dam pa dge 'dun dang//sangs
33b1(kha): rgyas zhabs la phyag 'tshal nas//tshangs pas 'di skad chig gsol te//gang dag sangs rgyas rang sangs rgyas//sangs rgyas kyi ni nyan thos dang //dang srang dang ni 'jig
33b2(kha): rten skyong//lha rnams ji snyed mchis pa dang//de kun mi yi 'jig rten ni//'di las skyes pa sha stag go//thub chen mnga' na 'tshal pa yi//gnod sbyin srin po 'di na mchis//
33b3(kha): rgyal po rnams kyis mthong mi nus//bstan par yang ni mi nus so//gang la bu ni mi chags dang//gang gi mngal du mi chags dang//skyes pa bud med 'du ba na//dbang po rnams
33b4(kha): ni myos par bgyid//mer mer po dang nur nur po//bu yi sa bon ma rungs bgyid//gang yang mngal ni thur du gyur//sha mi mi 'byung gnod par bgyid//de rnams kyi ni ming brjod kyi
33b5(kha): 'jig rten mgon po bdag la gso na//'jam pa po ni ri dgas rgyal//skem byed brjed byed khu tshur can//mi mo dang ni dza mi ka//'dod pa can dang nam gru dang//srul po dang ni
33b6(kha): dga' byed//bya dang gnya' ba'i lag can dang//gzhin rgyan dang ni mig 'phyang ba//de dag thams cad sa la rgyu//gdon rnams bcwo lnga de dag ni//byis pa rnams la 'jigs par bgyid
33b7(kha): ji ltar byis pa 'dzin pa yi//sa tshan dang gzugs kyang brjod par bgyi//'jam pa pos ni zin gyur na//mig ni rab tu 'byung bar bgyid//ri dgas rgyal gyis zin pa ni//skyugs
34a1(kha): @#//pa mi bzad skyugs par 'gyur//skye ma byed kyis ni zin gyur na//skye ma byed pas ni byis pa mgul//brjed byed kyis ni zan gyur na//'bre zhing de ltar sgra yang
34a2(kha): 'byin IIIIIIIIIIIII kha chu 'dzag/khu tshur can gyis rab zin pa//khu tshur 'chang zhing 'byed par 'gyed//ma mo yis ni zin gyur na//dag de cing de bzhin sgra yang 'byin//
34a3(kha): dza mi kas ni rab zin na//nu zho mthung bar mngon ma dga'//'dod pa can gyis rab zin na//mal na gnyid log du bar bgyid//nam grus rab tu zin gyur na//lI la sos ni 
34a4(kha): 'cha' bar bgyid//srul phos rab tu zin gyur na/ku co 'don cing sgra yang 'byin//ma dga' byed pas zin gyur na//rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs su 'gyur//bya yis rab tu zin 
34a5(kha): gyur na//rnal ba'i dri ni 'byung bar 'gyur//gnya' log can gyis zin gyur na//mgul pa rab tu dgags par 'gyur//gzhi ni rgyan gyis ni zin gyur na//rims kyis btab cing
34a6(kha): 'khrug par 'gyur//mig 'phyang bas ni zin gyur na//skyigs bu dang ni sngam pa 'byung//byis pa ji ltar skrag bgyid pa//de yi gzugs ni brjod par bgyi//'jam pa po ni ba lang
34a7(kha): gzugs//ri dgas rgyal po ri dgas 'dra//skyem byed pa ni gzhon nu'i gzugs//brjod byed pa ni wa 'dre'o//khu tshur can ni bya rog gzugs//ma mo de ni mi yi gzugs//dza mi
34b1(kha): /ni rta yi gzugs//'dod pa can ni rdo rje'i gzugs pa nam gru khyi'i gzugs kyis ste//srul po phag gi gzugs kyis so//ma dga' byed pa byi la'i gzugs//bya ni 'dab
34b2(kha): chags gzugs kyis so//gnya' lag can ni bya gag gzugs//gzhin rgyan 'ug pa'i gzugs su ste//mig 'byang ba ni pha bang gzugs//'di dag byes pa skrag par 
34b3(kha): bgyid//'di dag bu 'phrog ma rungs pa//byis pa rnams la 'jigs bgyid pa//mdo sde'i zhags pas drangs nas ni//'di dag shin du dgug par bgyi//gnod sbyin sde dgo
34b4(kha): chen po ni//dri za tsan dan zhes bgyi ba//yi ge phyag rgya de bstsal te//song la gdon ni drag po pa//bcwo lnga de dag khug shog ces//pho nyar yang na gtang bar bgyi//de dag rnams
34b5(kha): ni skad cig gis//bcing ba lnga yis bcings bzhin bkug/de nas 'jig rten mgon po la//tshangs chen thal sbyar 'di skad gsol//srog chags sa bon 'i jag bgyid pa//'gyur
34b6(kha): po 'di dag rnams lags te//mgon po yi ni spyan sngar ru//'di dag chad pa bcad par bgyi//bud med gang zhig bu mi 'tshal//gang zhig btsas pa 'dzin 'tshal bas//tshes
34b7(kha): brgyud dang ni bcu bzhi la//bsam bzung dam chos skyabs mchis la//mchod rten legs par mchod par bgyi//khrus bgyis shin du brgyan nas ni//sa bzhir yungs dkar
35a1(kha): @#//gtor nas su//mo tog spos kyis brgyan par bgyi//sna lnga'i tshon gyi skud pas ni//'khor yug tu ni bskor bar bgyi//nam phyed bar du mchis nas ni//spyi
35a2(kha): bor yungs dkar bzhag nas su//tshangs pas rab tu byas pa ste//tshangs pas sprul ces brjod par bgyi//lo grangs bcu gnyis bar du ni//gzhon nu rnams la phan bgyid
35a3(kha): pa/tshangs pas sprul pa'i mdo sde yi//rig sngags 'di la gang 'da' ba//a rdza ka'i dog pa bzhin//mgo bo tshal pa bdun du 'gas//s+yad ya thri dan//ang ge nang gi

36a1(kha): @#//rgya chen bgyis nas//rig sngags bklag par bgyi'o//tshes brgyad kyi tshe ni rgyal po chen po bzhi'i skyes bu dag/rgyal po chen po rnams kyi mdun na de la

36b2(kha): dang yo byad khyad par can gtsang mar bgyi/yul ngan pa rnyed par mi bgyi/yul ngan pa'i mdza'o bsten par mi bgyi/yul ngan ba na gnas pa la sngags par mi bgyi'o//gang

36b6(kha): bgyi ba'i 'os su 'gyur lags so//gang stong chen mo rab tu 'jos pa'i mdo 'di 'chang bar 'tshal na/rgyal po chen po bzhi yang gzhin ston par 'gyur na/gnod sbyin 
36b7(kha): dang srin po dul gi gnod la sogs kyang ci 'tshal/de ci'i slad du zhe na/sems can rnams la sman pa'i slad du 'jig rten na gang su dag/rig sngags bgyid pa de dag pas/
37a1(kha): @#//gso drug gos kyi tshig 'di dge rab mchog gtso bo//khyad par du 'phags pa dam pa zab pa rgya che ba/tshad ma mchis pa/'jug par dka' ba/thun mong ma lags

99b1(kha): khyod kyis bdag cag skyab cing 'jigs pa chen po las thar bar mdzad du gsol/de nas ded dpon chen po sems brtan pa/blo gros chen po des/tshong pa khrugs par gyur pa rnams la

107b3(kha): rgyas la|sdong bu dar gyis bcings pa bri//pad ma la ni rtse gsum pa//zur brgyad nas ni dar dpyangs bri//de bzhin pad ma dgra star bcas//'dab ma brgyad dang ldan par bri//pad mar bcas 
107b4(kha): pa'i ral gri bri//pad ma de ni dkar por bya//pad ma'i steng du dung yang bri//thams cad du yang rgyas par bya//kun du cho ga'i mtshan ma rnams//mkhas pa dag gis bri bar bya//gang la 
107b5(kha): gnod pa'i sems byung ba//byis pa'i gzugs rnams mi bri'o//sna tshogs brgyan gyi brgyan pa yi//lha yi gzugs ni bri bar bya//dge slong phyir ni rdo rje 'chang //gnod sbyin bsdigs 
107b6(kha): par brtson par bri//rgyal po chen po bzhi rnams kyang //ngos bzhi rnams su bri bar bya//brim ze'i phyir ni dbang ldan no//rgyal rigs la ni dbang phyug che//dmangs rigs phyir ni rtag bzhi 
107b7(kha): bya||khyab 'jug legs par dri bar bya//rje'u rigs phyir ni rnam thos bu//lha yi dbang phyug dbang po bri//khye'u'i phyir ni skye dgu'i bdag/blo gros chen po bri bar bya//sngo bsangs 
108a1(kha): @#//mdog ni gang yin pa//de yi ched du khros ba bra//dkar po'i phyir ni grags ldan pa//phun sum tshogs pa'i gzugs su bra//sbom po'i phyir ni nor bu bzang //rab tu sbrams te 
108a2(kha): bri bar bya//skem po'i phyir ni gang ba bzang //rang 'byung nga yis bstan pa bri//sbrum ma'i phyir ni nag mo che//tshangs lha rnams kyang bri bar bya//gzhan yang ji ltar cho ga las//gong du bshad 
108a3(kha): pa bzhin du bri//las kyi cho ga mthong nas ni//rab tu bsgrims te de bzhin bri//rtag tu mgul du btags na ni//de ni bde bar 'gyur ba yin//rgyal mtshan rtse mo'i pad ma la//yid bzhin 
108a4(kha): nor bu'i 'bar ba bri//pad ma'i snying po zhags pa dang //de bzhin 'khor lo bri bar bya//pad ma'i steng du rdo rje dang //pad ma'i snying por tho ba bri//cho ga las ni byung ba bzhin//pad 
108a5(kha): ma'i steng du mdung thung bri//cho ga las ni brjod pa bzhin//nor bu thams cad rtse mor 'bar//'od zer 'phro bas rgyas pa dang //dar gyis bcings pa bri bar bya//klu mgo 
108a6(kha): gdengs ka dgu yid la//nor bu rab tu 'bar ba dang //de dag kun gyi snying kar yang //rdo rje gnas pa sgrims te bri//rgyal po gnas phyir stobs can dang //ded dpon mkhas pas 
108a7(kha): rtag tu bri/rig pa 'dzin pa thams cad kyis//rig pa'i lha mo rnams kyang bri//zla ba nyi ma rgya skar bcas//sgra gcan 'jug rings gza' brgyad bri//cho ga bzhin du nges bris 
108b1(kha): la/blo gros ldan pa'i mis bsrel na//zam dang ni ma ning dang //bu pho rnyed par 'gyur ba yin//'di ni thams cad grub par byed//shes shing sdig pa zhi bar byed//rang 'byung gi ni bka' 
108b2(kha): bzhin du//gnas kyi dam pa thob par 'gyur//'jig rten 'dir yang mchog tu bde//'jig rten pha rol mchog tu bde//sum bcu rtsa gsum gnas la sogs//lha gnas de yi 
108b3(kha): gnas su 'gyur//'dzam gling bzang zhing nyams dga' bar//khyad par can gyi rigs grags dang //rgyal rigs khyad par 'phags pa dang //bram ze khyad par 'phags rnams su//bdag

108b7(kha): ba ltar 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur ba yin//mi dang lha rnams dbugs 'byin te//gnod par sems pa skrag par byed//gang phyir rig pa legs bshad pa//de ni ring por

121b7(kha): bgyid/che zhing gtam la mi bzang pa//ngag cing mtha' la mi sdig la//spu yang ring la sen mo ring//lag na ral gri tho ba thogs/so ni zangs can 'jigs par bgyid//sna chen rnga mo stag gi 'dra

122a3(kha): 'dra'i gnod sbyin mang po yi//g.yog 'khor dag gis yongs su bskor//gnod sbyin bye ba phrag stong rnams//de dag ma ni li ma log can//g.yog 'khor de dag lhan cig tu//bdag ni phyag

122b7(kha): yang rtsa ba ming zo ba dang/mnga' thang bsal zhing 'jigs par byed//glang chen mdog dang bum rna can//mgo bo stag dang seng ge 'dra//lcags kyi ska can lcags kyis //gdon rnams rnam par
123a1(kha): @#//ska gang kyang ngo bo//lag na lcags kyu gtun bu thogs//lag na lcags kyi tho ba thogs//so rnon lag pa ring ba ste//'jigs pa'i gzugs can lcags kyi mchu//mgo bo che la skra g.yang
